# Symply Health Acceptance Matrix

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| **Scope** | Symply Health (`symply-health`) — the nine-entry tab pool, plus the **parity-phase-P2 backend** (`FOOD` / `INJ` / `ASSET`) |
| **Prefix** | `HEALTH-` |
| **Status** | populated (Circle V2 density); 100%-coverage pass 2026-07-25; P2 backend groups (`FOOD` / `INJ` / `ASSET`) added 2026-07-25 |
| **Maestro app tag** | `app:health` |
| **Config** | `e2e/maestro/health/config.yaml` |
| **Contract** | [COVERAGE_CONTRACT.md](./COVERAGE_CONTRACT.md) |
| **Strategy** | [FLEET_TEST_STRATEGY.md §4.4](../FLEET_TEST_STRATEGY.md) |
| **Maestro inventory** | `e2e/maestro/health/*.yaml`, `e2e/maestro/health/subflows/` |

### Section → Maestro `feature:*` tag

| Section | Tag | Screens / overlap |
|---------|-----|-------------------|
| HOME | `feature:home` | `HealthHomeScreen` |
| MORE | `feature:more` | `HealthMoreScreen` |
| PRIV | `feature:privacy` | Home privacy card + More privacy rows + cross-user/on-device posture |
| SCROLL | `feature:scroll` | Home + More scroll contracts |
| AUTH | `feature:auth` | Health-branded login (no session) |
| API | n/a (no Maestro) | `symply-health-api` Worker contract — `backend/__tests__/live/health-live-api.mjs` |
| SPIN | n/a (Jest) | `HealthSpinner` + brand-aware `ActivityIndicator` shim |
| STORE | n/a (Jest) | `healthLocalStorage.ts` MMKV keys |
| ROUTE | n/a (Jest) | `app/(tabs)/index.tsx` + `settings.tsx` brand swaps |
| WIDGET | n/a (Jest + Swift) | `widget_health_today` handoff, `SymplyHealthWidgetContent`, brand routing |
| WATCH | n/a (Jest + Swift) | `watch_health_today` handoff, `SymplyHealthWatchView` |
| CYCLE | `feature:cycle` | `HealthCycleScreen` (Women's Health) — `defaultHidden`, reached via the More hub |
| VITALITY | `feature:vitality` | `HealthVitalityScreen` (Men's Health) — `defaultHidden`, reached via the More hub |
| GOALS | `feature:goals` | `HealthGoalsScreen` (route `health-goals`), reached from More → Preferences → "Goals and targets". Added to this table 2026-07-31 — the screen and its two Maestro flows predate this row, but no section, TOC entry or tag mapping existed for it until this pass; see Flagged (P1) |
| EXER | `feature:exercises` | `HealthWorkoutLibraryScreen` + `HealthExerciseDetailScreen` (the modal it opens) — `defaultHidden`, reached via the More hub. Catalogue seeded by `backend/migrations/0123_health_exercise_library.sql`; "log this" writes through the Activity store |
| WEIGHT | `feature:weight` | `HealthWeightScreen` (donor `WeightTabView` + `WeightDashboardView`) — `defaultHidden`, reached via the More hub. The goal columns need `backend/migrations/0125_health_weight_goal.sql`, which is written but NOT applied and NOT deployed |
| BODY | `feature:body` | `HealthBodyScreen` + `HealthBodyProgress` (trend + compare cards) — `defaultHidden`, reached via the More hub |
| FOOD | `feature:food` / `feature:recipes` (backend vitest for `001…052`) | P2 `/health` food surface — `backend/src/routes/health-food.ts`, `backend/src/routes/__tests__/health-food.test.ts` — plus its two screens, `HealthFoodLibraryScreen` (`feature:food`) and `HealthRecipesScreen` (`feature:recipes`), both `defaultHidden` and reached via the More hub |
| FRIDGE | `feature:fridge` | `HealthFridgeScreen` (donor "Smart Fridge") — `defaultHidden`, reached via the More hub. Worker contract is under ASSET |
| CHAL | `feature:challenges` | `HealthChallengesScreen` + `healthChallengesStorage` over `/health/challenges` (donor `ChallengesSettingsView`). Added 2026-08-13 — the surface had **no** Jest suite and **no** Maestro flow of any kind before that pass. Unlike every other row in this table it is **not** flag-gated: `health-challenges` is absent from `src/config/healthFeatures.ts`, so it is not behind `needs-admin-feature-seed` — and it owns no tab either, pinned or hidden. Reached by push from the Home FOOD CHALLENGES widget's pencil (`subflows/go-health-challenges.yaml`) or the Nutrition tab's Today's-Challenges card |
| INJ | n/a (backend vitest + Jest) | P2 `/health` body-extras surface — `backend/src/routes/health-body-extras.ts`, `backend/src/routes/__tests__/health-body-extras.test.ts`. `001` … `040` are the Worker contract; `041` … `073` are the RN **client** store `src/features/health/healthInjuryStorage.ts` |
| INJUI | n/a (Jest) | `HealthInjuriesScreen` — the donor injury tracker (`BodyPainTrackerView`), `defaultHidden` and reached via the More hub. The WRITE half of the workout gate scored under EXER: `/health/injuries*` is what `/health/exercises` reads to compute `injury_flag` |
| AI | n/a (backend vitest + Jest) | P3 `/health/ai/*` — the AI coach, the label + meal scanners and the body-insight producer. `backend/src/routes/health-ai.ts` + `backend/src/services/health-ai/**`, plus the RN client `src/api/healthAi.ts`, `src/features/health/healthCoachStorage.ts` and the two screens `HealthCoachScreen` / `HealthScanScreen` (both `defaultHidden`, reached via the More hub). **No Maestro flow yet** — `e2e/maestro/health/` is owned elsewhere |
| ASSET | n/a (backend vitest) | P2 `/health` assets surface — `backend/src/routes/health-assets.ts`, `backend/src/routes/__tests__/health-assets.test.ts` |
| TAB | `feature:home` / `feature:more` | Two-tab shell |
| FLAG | n/a (Jest) | Per-feature admin toggles — `src/config/healthFeatures.ts` (catalog), `src/stores/healthFeatureStore.ts` (resolver), `src/hooks/useHealthFeature.ts`, `src/features/health/screens/HealthFeaturesScreen.tsx` (the admin switchboard, `/health-features`), `src/features/health/components/HealthFeatureRoute.tsx` (per-route guard). `HEALTH-FLAG-001…047` in `src/features/health/__tests__/areas/platform.featureFlags.test.ts`. **No Maestro flow yet**: the admin-vs-common-user split needs a second seeded account (`users.role = 'admin'`, migration `0135`) that the fleet's shared E2E login does not currently mint — see `shared-test-account` |

### Tiers for native (WIDGET / WATCH)

| Tier | What it covers | Runnable today |
|------|----------------|----------------|
| **Tier 1** | Data-contract rows — snapshot keys, field schema, auth gating, brand-key derivation, clear-allowlist. Asserted from **Jest** by reading the Swift sources, matching the existing `widget-sync-app-group.test.ts` pattern | ✅ yes — no Xcode build |
| **Tier 2** | Pure-function and layout rows — formatters, tints, family selection, timeline policy | ⚠️ watch: needs `scripts/run-watch-tests.sh` (target + scheme exist, not in CI). **Widget: the `SymplyEcosystemWidgetTests` target now exists** (`xcodebuild test -scheme SymplyEcosystemWidgetTests`); `KaizenWidgetDataTests` + `WidgetSignInGateTests` run green. Remaining planned Tier-2 files (formatters/layout/link) are still to be written |

## §0 Test account prerequisites

| Requirement | Detail |
|-------------|--------|
| Brand | `symply-health` (`com.symply.health`, scheme `simplehealth://`) |
| Tabs | **Health** (`index`) + **More** (`settings`) — no other shipped tabs |
| Backend | Health's **own** Worker `symply-health-api` (`backend/wrangler.health.toml`) with **isolated** D1 / R2 / KV — *not* the House Worker. Health is a `isJoinedPlatformBrand()` brand, so it shares the **route contract** (`/auth/*`, `/users/me`, `/households`) but never the House data plane. Base URLs: `HEALTH_STAGING_API_URL` / `HEALTH_PRODUCTION_API_URL` (`src/config/env.ts`). |
| Network surface | Auth + profile + notifications only. **Every health log (weight / water / note / prefs) is MMKV-local and issues no request** — see PRIV-018. ⚠️ **Stale since P1** — the health domain is now backend-backed (`backend/src/routes/health.ts`, migration 0119) with MMKV demoted to an offline read-through cache. See [Flagged](#product--code-defects-surfaced-by-this-audit-fix-dont-just-test). |
| Backend (P2) | Three further routers on the **same** `symply-health-api` Worker, all mounted at `/health` behind `requireHealthApi()` and all **user-scoped, never household-scoped**: `health-food.ts` (custom foods, `/foods/search`, `/foods/suggestions`, recipes + scaling), `health-body-extras.ts` (injuries, `/injuries/active-body-parts`, activity preferences, body insights), `health-assets.ts` (files on R2, widget preferences + `/widget/snapshot`, fridge). Schema: `backend/migrations/0120_health_p2.sql` — 10 tables. Groups 40–42 need **no** simulator, **no** session and **no** network: `npm --prefix backend test -- health-food health-body-extras health-assets` |
| Credentials | `e2e/credentials.local` — primary `a.tekhtelev@gmail.com`, secondary `andrei.tekhytelev@gmail.com`. Seed via `npm run test:e2e:seed:fleet` (copies House user + `password_hash` into Health D1). |
| Launch | `e2e/maestro/health/subflows/launch-health.yaml` |
| Login subflow | `e2e/maestro/health/subflows/health-login-if-needed.yaml` |
| Local data | Weight/water/note/prefs via `healthLocalStorage.ts` → MMKV keys `health.weightLog.v1`, `health.water.v1`, `health.notes.v1`, `health.prefs.v1`. Keys are **global, not per-user** — see PRIV-011. Prefer a fresh sim (or explicit key wipe) for empty-state rows. |
| Device | iOS Simulator with Symply Health build; Metro `:8081` with `APP_BRAND=symply-health` |
| Privacy stance | No HealthKit, no cloud health sync, no AI coach in shell — rows assert **OFF / not connected** copy and prove the OFF states are invariants, not defaults |
| Runner | Maestro suite via `e2e/maestro/health/config.yaml` (serial; `login-screen-controls` first) |

Backend-backed (`recipes`). Self-sufficient: mints its OWN ingredient on the Foods tab and deletes it in teardown, so it never inherits group 44's leftovers. Backend-backed (`fridge_items`; Worker contract is `HEALTH-ASSET-033` … `043`). Stocks four urgency buckets — expired / use today / this week / no date — and removes all four. Relative dates come from the quick picks: Maestro cannot do date arithmetic |

Deletes both seed rows |

### Home dashboard widgets — 2026-07-29 Maestro pass (previously zero coverage at any layer)

`HealthDayRings`, `HealthDashboardCards`, `HealthHomeMetricSheet`/`buildHomeMetricDetail`, `HealthCalorieRingCard`, `HealthWeeklyTrendsWidget` and `HealthFoodChallengesWidget` had no Maestro testID driven anywhere in the suite and no Jest test file importing any of the six components before this pass — confirmed by grepping every flow and every `__tests__` directory. All six ship in `DEFAULT_HOME_WIDGETS`, so this is regression coverage for every fresh account, not an opt-in surface.

## ONB — Onboarding NUX (Health goals mini-flow)

`ChildWelcomeScreen` → the five-step GOALS mini-flow (`HealthGoalsBiometricsScreen` "About you" → `HealthGoalsWeightScreen` → `HealthGoalsNutritionScreen` → `HealthGoalsActivityScreen` → `HealthGoalsWaterScreen`, all built on the shared `OnboardingStepScreen`) → `EssentialPermissionsScreen` (notifications + Health's own reminder toggles) → `HealthKitPermissionScreen` → Home.

**Reordered** so About-you (gender/age/height/activity level) comes FIRST rather than last: `suggestGoals()` needs all five of weight/height/age/gender/activity-level to produce a personalised calorie/step/water suggestion (`missingSuggestionInputs`), and asking for the biometrics before Weight/Nutrition/Activity/Water means each of those later steps can show a real "Suggested for you" banner (`useSuggestedGoals()`) instead of a blank field. Water also gained a 4-unit switcher (mL/L/cups/fl oz, `HEALTH-ONB-027`) whose choice now syncs server-side (`health_goals.water_unit`, migration 0140) rather than staying device-local.

Had **zero** device coverage before the wave that added rows 001-018 — only Jest unit tests per screen.

**New this wave, not yet device-executed.** `e2e/maestro/health/onboarding.yaml` is a SOFT, session-preserving launch (no `clearState`/`clearKeychain`/logout), shaped after `language/onboarding.yaml` rather than House's `launch-onboarding.yaml`: the shared E2E account is already onboarded on most sims, so the flow's Path A (the real walkthrough) only fires against a genuinely fresh account, and Path B (already onboarded) just confirms Home. Every `id:` selector it drives is confirmed to exist in the real rendered source (`HEALTH-SUITE-002`, `e2e/__tests__/healthSuiteCompleteness.test.ts`) and it is scheduled in `flowsOrder` (`HEALTH-SUITE-003`) — but neither of those runs a simulator, so "runs green end-to-end on a fresh account" itself is unverified pending an actual device run.

## API — `symply-health-api` Worker contract

Health's **own** Worker (`backend/wrangler.health.toml`, isolated D1 / R2 / KV). Exercised by `backend/__tests__/live/health-live-api.mjs` (node, hits **real** staging + production). Read-only and session-free, so this whole section is **runnable today** — unlike every simulator group.

| ID | Description | Steps | Expected | UI elements | BE / persistence | Console verify | Layer | Automation | Notes |
|----|-------------|-------|----------|-------------|------------------|----------------|-------|------------|-------|
| HEALTH-API-001 | Staging health check | 1. `GET /health` against `HEALTH_STAGING_API_URL` | **200** with a healthy status body; Worker reachable | n/a | `GET /health` | status 200 | Live | `backend/__tests__/live/health-live-api.mjs` | **LOAD** |
| HEALTH-API-002 | Production health check | 1. `GET /health` against `HEALTH_PRODUCTION_API_URL` | **200**; production Worker deployed and reachable | n/a | `GET /health` | status 200 | Live | `backend/__tests__/live/health-live-api.mjs` | **LOAD** |
| HEALTH-API-003 | Public registration is OPEN on Health | 1. `POST /auth/register` with a fresh email | **201** with a user row plus an access/refresh pair. Health's Worker allows self-signup — the matrix previously claimed the opposite, but the live suite has asserted 201 since it was written | n/a | `POST /auth/register` | status 201 | Live | `backend/__tests__/live/health-live-api.mjs` | **CORRECTED 2026-07-25** — the old "registration disabled" row was stale and contradicted its own citation |
| HEALTH-API-004 | Wrong password → clean 401 | 1. `POST /auth/login` with a real email and a wrong password | **401** with `error.code = unauthorized` and an `invalid email or password` message — never a hint about which half was wrong | n/a | `POST /auth/login` | status 401 | Live | `backend/__tests__/live/health-live-api.mjs` | **CORRECTED 2026-07-25** — the shared account IS seeded now (`npm run test:e2e:seed:fleet`), so the old "unseeded → 401" framing no longer describes the system; see API-020 |
| HEALTH-API-005 | Malformed email → 400 | 1. `POST /auth/login` with `{ email: 'not-an-email' }` | **400** validation error, not 401 and not 500 — input is validated before any credential lookup | n/a | `POST /auth/login` | status 400 | Live | `backend/__tests__/live/health-live-api.mjs` | **VALIDATION** — server side of AUTH-010 |
| HEALTH-API-006 | `/users/me` requires auth | 1. `GET /users/me` with no `Authorization` header | **401**; no user payload leaks in the body | n/a | `GET /users/me` | status 401 | Live | `backend/__tests__/live/health-live-api.mjs` | **ERROR** / security negative |
| HEALTH-API-007 | `/households` requires auth | 1. `GET /households` with no `Authorization` header | **401**; no household data returned to an anonymous caller | n/a | `GET /households` | status 401 | Live | `backend/__tests__/live/health-live-api.mjs` | **ERROR** / security negative |

Confirm D1 `symply-health-db-*`, R2 `symply-health-reports-*` and per-env queues are Health-specific | Health binds **no** House D1/R2/KV. `APP_BRAND=symply-health`, `JWT_ISSUER=symply-health`, `JWT_AUDIENCE=symply-health-app` — a House-issued token is not valid here | n/a | `wrangler.health.toml` bindings | n/a | Live | `backend/__tests__/live/health-live-api.mjs` | **CORNER** — the isolation this matrix's privacy story depends on |

## SPIN — Branded loading indicator

`HealthSpinner` (`src/features/health/components/HealthSpinner.tsx`) is the ONLY spinner the Health brand renders: `src/components/ui/ActivityIndicator.tsx` swaps it in for `symply-health` and the lime→teal `SymplySpinner` for every other brand. It sits on the Home loading gate (HOME-002) and on every shared AI/download surface Health inherits. Both files had **zero** tests before 2026-07-25 (HealthSpinner sat at 20% statements, 0% branches).

Reanimated is replaced by a JS mock in `jest.setup.js`, so the animation drivers are asserted through the reanimated API rather than by sampling frames.

## WATCH — Symply Health watch glance

`SymplyHealthWatchView` is a rendering shell selected by the same brand router as the widget. It reads a **different key with a different schema** from the widget, and that key has **no writer anywhere in the repo** — so the Health watch glance is empty by construction today. Rows below pin both the intended contract and the current defect.

### The diary's PER-ROW verbs, on a device (`nutrition-meal-detail-tools.yaml`)

`nutrition-log-meal.yaml` drives the ADD FOOD form and the day stepper and
nothing else, so the diary's largest control cluster had never been touched on a
device: `HealthMealDetail` 1 of 15 controls, `HealthQuickAdd` 0 of 7. Everything
a member does to a row they have **already** logged — edit, re-portion, move,
delete — was unproven outside Jest.

Two selector rules make these rows expressible, and both are load-bearing:

* **id AND label together.** A row's testID carries the server-minted entry id
  (`health-meal-edit-n_a10e1b0f-…`), which no flow can know in advance, and a
  bare `health-meal-edit-.*` also matches `health-meal-edit-name-…`,
  `-calories-…` and `-save-…` once the editor is open, because Maestro treats an
  id as a whole-string regex. Every per-row tap pins both the id pattern and the
  row's accessibility label, which are properties of the same element — verified
  against a live view-hierarchy dump (`resource-id` + `accessibilityText`).
* **The names are swept on entry.** A run that dies mid-flight leaves its rows
  behind, so the next run logs a second row with the same name and every
  label-qualified tap becomes ambiguous. Observed for real: two `Maestro Diary
  One` rows, 520 kcal, from two aborted runs. `subflows/health-clear-maestro-meals.yaml`
  deletes only rows whose name starts with `Maestro `, so a member's own row on
  the shared account is never touched.

### The diary's BATCH verbs, on a device (`nutrition-copy-and-batch.yaml`)

`HealthMealTools` was 3 of 12 controls driven, and the two deployed routes behind
it — `POST /nutrition/copy-day` and `POST /nutrition/entries/bulk` — had never
been exercised from a device at all. Split from the per-row flow deliberately:
one flow covering both would be long enough that a mid-run driver drop would cost
the whole diary surface, and the two halves fail for different reasons.

The seed lives on **yesterday** because a copy needs a source day that is not the
target, and the only way a run can produce one is to step the diary back and log
there — the app has no back-dating field. The selection's "Copy to…" targets
yesterday rather than another slot on today so the copies land off the screen
being asserted, keeping every row name unique.

### 0124 — a diary row remembers where it came from, and can be re-portioned

Migration `0124_health_entry_updates.sql` added `nutrition_entries.food_id` and the donor's four `base_*_per_100` columns (donor `018_nutrition_base_values.sql`). Before them a food logged from the library stored only the resulting absolute macros, so changing the portion afterwards had nothing to rescale FROM.

### Copy + batch delete — the deployed routes that had no client

`POST /health/nutrition/copy-day` and `POST /health/nutrition/entries/bulk` shipped
to staging **and** production with **zero RN callers** (UI_PARITY_AUDIT §1, §10.1),
so "same dinner as yesterday" cost N manual entries. Both now have one.
`HealthCopyPanel` collapses three donor sheets — `CopyMealFromDateSheet`,
`CopyMealToMealSheet` and a whole-day copy the donor never had — into a single
request each, because `copy-day` takes `from_slot` **and** `to_slot`.

There is **no bulk DELETE route**, so `deleteMealEntries` is a client-side batch
over the per-entry route. It differs from the donor's `deleteEntries` twice on
purpose: every id is attempted (the donor abandoned the rest at the first throw)
and a partial result is *reported* (the donor also skipped its own reload, so the
rows it had deleted stayed on screen with one generic alert).

| ID | Description | Steps | Expected | UI elements | BE / persistence | Console verify | Layer | Automation | Notes |
|----|-------------|-------|----------|-------------|------------------|----------------|-------|------------|-------|
| HEALTH-NUTR-140 | copy-day sends the wire body with the `snack` alias on BOTH slots | 1. `copyMealsFromDay` with `fromSlot`/`toSlot` `snacks` | `{ from_date, to_date, from_slot: 'snack', to_slot: 'snack' }`. The app's plural would be rejected by the route's zod enum on either end | n/a (store) | `POST /health/nutrition/copy-day` | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/health/__tests__/healthNutritionStorage.test.ts` | **INTERACT** |
| HEALTH-NUTR-141 | A copy APPENDS at the target and leaves the source alone | 1. Dinner on both days 2. Copy yesterday's dinner into today | Today holds both items; yesterday is unchanged. The donor's copy sheets never delete at the target, and a copy that wiped a slot would be unrecoverable | meal groups | `POST /health/nutrition/copy-day` | n/a (local) | Unit | same | **INTERACT** |
| HEALTH-NUTR-142 | An empty source is "nothing to copy", not a silent success | 1. Copy a meal that has nothing in it | `status: 'nothing-to-copy'` and copy naming the day. The route answers 201 with zero rows, so an error-only check would confirm nothing | banner | `POST /health/nutrition/copy-day` | n/a (local) | Unit | same | **CORNER** — honest empty |
| HEALTH-NUTR-143 | A same-day slot→slot copy is ONE request | 1. ## ACT — Activity tab (donor "Workouts")

`HealthActivityScreen` + `healthActivityStorage` — workout sessions (type + duration + burned kcal), a manually-entered daily step count, movement/step goals and a 7-day summary. **HealthKit is not read here**: the migration plan requires a working HealthKit-OFF path, so steps are typed in until a scoped, privacy-reviewed Apple Health phase lands.

### 0124 — a session can be EDITED, and intensity is a real column

Migration `0124_health_entry_updates.sql` closed two gaps this tab had been working around: `/health/entries` was create + soft-delete only (so an edit RE-RECORDED, moving the logged time), and a workout had no intensity (so it rode the note as a `[hard]` tag).

## EXER — Workout library tab (donor `exercise_library`)

`HealthWorkoutLibraryScreen` + `HealthExerciseDetailScreen` + `healthExerciseStorage` — the 88-row exercise catalogue seeded by `backend/migrations/0123_health_exercise_library.sql`, with search, four bands of derived filter chips, favourites, and a detail sheet that can log a session.

Two properties are load-bearing and shape every row below:

* **The injury verdict is the SERVER's.** `injury_flag` / `injury_body_parts` arrive already computed from the account's active injuries; the screen renders and de-prioritises by them and never decides for itself. There is **no injury-logging surface in the app**, so the flagged paths are asserted through a conditional leg that skips cleanly on a clear account — see [Harness gaps](#harness-gaps).
* **The detail sheet is a MODAL, not a route**, precisely so the query, the filters and the scroll position survive behind it. `HEALTH-EXER-112` is that guarantee.

Row ids (`ex_plank`, `ex_box_breathing`, `ex_legs_up_wall`) are authored in the migration, not generated per user, which is what makes them safe to tap by testID.

| ID | Description | Steps | Expected | UI elements | BE / persistence | Console verify | Layer | Automation | Notes |
|----|-------------|-------|----------|-------------|------------------|----------------|-------|------------|-------|
| HEALTH-EXER-101 | The library opens through the More hub | 1. ## TREND — Trends tab (donor "Weight Stats", widened)

## WEIGHT — Weight tab (donor `WeightTabView` + `WeightDashboardView`)

`HealthWeightScreen` + `healthWeightAnalytics` + `healthWeightStorage` +
`HealthWeightWidgets` — the donor's dedicated Weight tab and its fourteen-widget
"Weight Stats" dashboard, which the RN app previously had no home for at all
(weight lived as a logging card on Home and a summary card on Trends).

Four rules are load-bearing here:

* **A window is a calendar span, and it can be walked BACKWARDS.** Every range
  picker in this app used to be trailing-only, so a reading from last March was
  unreachable.
* **Never mix units.** The series plots one unit; the GOAL is stored in canonical
  kilograms and converted once, at the edge, for display and for the rule.
* **A truncated y-axis is disclosed in words.** A weight series lives in a narrow
  band far from zero, so the line chart offers a floor — and always says where it
  starts.
* **Refuse rather than guess.** No projection under 5 readings, no correlation
  under 4 weeks, no BMI without a height, no TDEE without an activity level.

**Backend note:** the goal columns land in `backend/migrations/0125_health_weight_goal.sql`,
which is WRITTEN BUT NOT APPLIED and NOT DEPLOYED. Until it is, `PUT /goals`
strips the keys and answers 200 — the client tells that server generation apart
from "the member cleared it" by KEY PRESENCE (`HEALTH-WEIGHT-210`…`213`).

| ID | Description | Steps | Expected | UI elements | BE / persistence | Console verify | Layer | Automation | Notes |
|----|-------------|-------|----------|-------------|------------------|----------------|-------|------------|-------|

| HEALTH-WEIGHT-101 | Past windows are reachable | 1. Offset 4 on a 30-day width | 14 Feb – 15 Mar — a span a trailing-only range could never show | window navigator | none | n/a (local) | Unit | same | **INTERACT** — the gap this closes |

## BODY — Body tab (donor "Body", measurements only)

`HealthBodyScreen` + `healthBodyStorage` + `HealthBodyProgress` — **fourteen** measurement sites with cm-or-in units and per-metric history, grouped the way the donor's manual-entry form groups them (Upper body / Arms / Legs / Composition), each length site with its own trend chart over the donor's 1M/3M/6M/1Y/All ranges, plus body fat on its own `%` axis and a two-date compare. Left and right limbs are tracked separately — asymmetry is a real signal. **Body photos are deliberately absent**: the migration matrix files them under "Later — future Health media spec" because they need their own storage, retention and deletion review.

## HABIT — Habits tab (donor "Habits")

`HealthHabitsScreen` + `healthHabitsStorage` — a starter habit set, custom habits, a daily tick, streaks and a 7-day strip. A habit stores the **days** it was completed on, so streaks are recomputed rather than counted — they survive an untick, a gap, and a device clock change.

## CYCLE — Women's Health tab

`HealthCycleScreen` + `healthCycleStorage` — the donor's `WomensHealthView`: period days with a flow level, a daily mood / energy / symptom / craving log, and calendar-arithmetic predictions for the next period and fertile window. Positioning is **wellness, not clinical** — the predictions come from the user's own logged dates and the screen says so, so the disclaimer is a scored row, not decoration.

The tab ships `defaultHidden` in `brands/symply-health/brand.cjs`, so the only way in is the More hub's **MORE TABS** section — the E2E rows enter through `subflows/go-health-cycle.yaml`, never a `tab-health-cycle` that does not exist.

Two states are **not re-runnable preconditions** and are therefore asserted as two-branch invariants: removing a period day RE-ANCHORS rather than leaving `last_period_start` where it was — it moves to the last remaining run start, or to null when nothing is left (`removePeriodDay` in `backend/src/services/health-service.ts`; pinned by `HEALTH-CYCLE-120…124`) — so the "no anchor" empty state depends on what else is logged; and a scale has no "unset", so mood/energy stay logged for the day. Both are covered by asserting *exactly one* branch holds rather than by a precondition that can pass only once.

Unit coverage for `healthCycleStorage` (cycle math, prediction roll-forward, severity cycling, corrupt-row guards) **now exists** — `src/features/health/__tests__/healthCycleStorage.test.ts` runs green inside the 44-suite Health run. Numbered `001…` / `050…` rows for it have still not been written into this table; that backfill is the outstanding half, not the tests.

## VITALITY — Men's Health tab

`HealthVitalityScreen` + `healthVitalityStorage` — the donor's `MensHealthView`: one upserted entry per day covering drive, activity and satisfaction, erection health, issues, energy / clarity / mood / sleep / stress and kegel sets, scored with the donor's exact vitality formula. Wellness framing, not diagnosis — the donor's disclaimer card is ported and scored.

The tab ships `defaultHidden`, so the E2E rows enter through the More hub (`subflows/go-health-vitality.yaml`), never a `tab-health-vitality`.

**Two rows are conditional by design** — Satisfaction only renders once there was activity, Morning quality only once a morning erection was recorded. Their negatives are asserted with a neighbouring row proven on screen, so "not visible" means *absent*, not *below the fold*.

There is **no delete affordance** for a day's entry (the screen upserts it), so the flow's teardown resets every **toggle** it flipped: a toggle left on would be inverted by the next run. Scales are left where they are — re-tapping the same level is idempotent.

Unit coverage for `healthVitalityStorage` (the score formula, clamps, trend window) **now exists** — `src/features/health/__tests__/healthVitalityStorage.test.ts` runs green inside the 44-suite Health run. Numbered `001…` / `050…` rows for it have still not been written into this table; that backfill is the outstanding half, not the tests. Two of those formulas are now also pinned ON A DEVICE, because `energyScore` and `mentalScore` are pure functions of controls on this screen — see `HEALTH-VITALITY-130`/`131`.

## GOALS — Goals & targets screen (`HealthGoalsScreen`)

**Added 2026-07-31 — closing a real zero-coverage gap this matrix's own Revision
log had twice claimed was closed.** The 2026-07-26 and 2026-07-28 entries below
describe `HEALTH-GOALS-101…150` and `123…129` as shipped, scored rows; neither
range was ever actually written into this document — no section header, no TOC
entry, no Execution-Groups row, and zero `HEALTH-GOALS-*` lines in any table
existed before this pass. The screen (`HealthGoalsScreen.tsx`, route
`health-goals`, reached from More → Preferences → "Goals and targets") and both
Maestro flows the old entries describe are real and on disk — this was a
documentation gap, not a fabricated feature — but the coverage they represent
was invisible to anyone reading this matrix rather than the repo. See Flagged
for the severity note and a second defect found while closing this: the Jest
suite for this screen independently embeds its own `HEALTH-GOALS-1xx` numbers
in `it()` descriptions, and they collide with (and mean something different
from) the Maestro `E2E_MATRIX_ROW` tags of the same number. This section adopts
the **Maestro** numbering as authoritative, matching how every other section in
this file keeps one numeric block per layer (e.g. `ACT` 001…024 store /
050…057 screen / 101+ E2E) — the Jest file's own 100s numbering is the
pre-existing defect, not reproduced here.

One server row (`health_goals`) backs Nutrition/Activity/Water/Weight; this
screen is the only writer for four of its fields and the sole writer for the
per-weekday calorie AND macro plans. Saving is per-card (four separate
`PUT /health/goals` round trips), not one button, because the writes are
genuinely separate and a single "Save" would have to report four outcomes as
one. Jest coverage exists and is substantial (`HealthGoalsScreen.test.tsx`, 47
cases; `healthGoalsStorage.test.ts`, 69 cases) — cited generically in
Automation below, not enumerated 1:1, per this matrix's "proportionate rows"
rule; the two Maestro flows below are the device-facing half.

## FOOD — custom foods, search, recipes (P2 backend)

`backend/src/routes/health-food.ts` + `backend/src/services/health-food-service.ts` — the donor's `customFoods.ts`, `foodSearch.ts` and `recipes.ts`, mounted at `/health` on the `symply-health-api` Worker behind `requireHealthApi()`. Tables come from `backend/migrations/0120_health_p2.sql` (`custom_foods`, `recipes`, `food_usage_history`).

Exercised by `backend/src/routes/__tests__/health-food.test.ts` — **55 vitest cases** against miniflare D1, so the whole section is **runnable today** with no simulator and no network: `npm --prefix backend test -- health-food`.

Health data is **personal**: every row is scoped to the authenticated user and there is no household read path (BRD §7). Two properties this section exists to protect:

- **The per-100g basis is the source of truth.** A serving is never stored as the client sent it — it is re-derived from `base_*_per_100` on every write and every use. Rescaling the previously *rounded* serving instead compounds error: re-portioning 100 → 7 → 3 → 250 → 100 g lands on 155.67 kcal instead of the original 155.5 (FOOD-014). Three hops is enough to invent 0.17 kcal, and nothing downstream would ever notice.
- **Recipe totals are computed server-side and the client's are ignored.** A client that posts `total_calories: 99999` is answered with the server's 229.4 (FOOD-041), and `/scale` never writes, so scaling twice cannot compound (FOOD-052).

Deny-by-default runs through the whole surface: the brand gate 404s before auth on every non-Health Worker, cross-user access is 404 and never 403, and deletes are soft so the tombstone can carry on the sync cursor.

**The screens landed on 2026-07-25, mid-pass** — `HealthFoodLibraryScreen` and `HealthRecipesScreen`, routed at `app/(tabs)/health-food.tsx` and `health-recipes.tsx`, both `defaultHidden` in the brand pack and reachable through the More hub (MORE-060/061). **Both are now driven end to end** by `food-library-manage.yaml` (`101`, `102`, `105 … 112`) and `recipes-build-and-scale.yaml` (`103`, `113 … 120`), added 2026-07-25 the same day.

Only **`HEALTH-FOOD-104` remains `PLANNED`**, and for a reason worth recording rather than papering over: the UI cannot express it. FOOD-014 re-portions by PUTting `portion` **alone** and letting the Worker re-derive the serving from the stored basis, but `HealthFoodLibraryScreen.handleSave` always posts the whole draft — portion *and* calories together — so changing the portion field in the app changes the basis instead of re-deriving from it. The round trip therefore has no user-reachable form today. Either the screen gains a portion-only edit affordance, or the row stays unscored; inventing a flow that types both fields would assert something FOOD-014 does not claim.

### P3 — the external food database (FatSecret)

The donor's `foodSearch.ts` topped its search up from FatSecret; P2 shipped the
library half only, so **there was no way to look up a food the user had not
already typed in themselves**. This section covers the port.

Three properties carry most of the weight, and each has its own cluster below:

1. **The mapping is the product.** FatSecret sends numbers as strings, sends
   `servings.serving` as an array *or* an object, and often omits the metric
   serving size. The third one, mishandled, is a wrong `base_*_per_100` in a
   user's library — and every portion of that food, forever after, derives from
   it. The donor got it wrong (`FOOD-203`), so these are asserted against exact
   numbers rather than shapes.
2. **Every failure is a word, never a string.** `not_configured` /
   `rate_limited` / `unavailable` / `skipped` each reach the UI as their own
   sentence. `FOOD-229` asserts the negative directly: no provider body, no HTTP
   code, no host name and no credential appears in a response.
3. **A hit the user keeps becomes a row they own.** That is what makes it work
   offline and keeps `base_*_per_100` re-portioning applicable to it —
   `FOOD-230`, `FOOD-237`.

**No test touches the real FatSecret API.** `globalThis.fetch` is stubbed and
every request recorded, which is also how `FOOD-212` ("the secret only ever goes
to the token endpoint") is expressible at all.

State at the time of writing: **credential not set, migration `0127` applied to
no D1, nothing deployed.** The suites cover the shipping behaviour of that state
too — `FOOD-210`, `FOOD-227`, `FOOD-242`, `FOOD-252` are all the
"not configured" path, which is what a user would see today.

Junk (`'n/a'`, `NaN`, absent) is 0, never `NaN` — a `NaN` basis would poison every portion derived from it and render as blank rather than wrong | n/a | `toNumber` | exact numbers | API | `backend/src/services/__tests__/health-food-provider.test.ts` | **CORNER** — the provider's wire format is strings |

### `HealthFoodLibraryScreen` — 2026-07-29 Maestro pass, closes two named gaps from the 2026-07-26 coverage table

`food-library-manage.yaml` already drives 17/22 of this screen's controls. The two remaining reachable gaps — `health-food-portion-input`, `health-food-suggestion-*` — close here. The two external-database row verbs (`health-food-external-*`, `HEALTH-FOOD-254…257` above) stay undriven: `FATSECRET_CLIENT_ID`/`FATSECRET_CLIENT_SECRET` are not set on any Health deploy (confirmed against `backend/src/services/health-food-provider.ts`'s own header), so `/foods/search` always answers `not_configured` and there is nothing live to drive, not merely something hard to schedule.

### Recipe → diary — the last unbuilt join in the food area

`grep -c 'logFoodToDiary\|addMealEntry' HealthRecipesScreen.tsx` was **0**
(UI_PARITY_AUDIT §2, backlog item 3): a user could build a recipe and then had no
way to eat it. `logRecipeToDiary` closes it, and the scaling maths stays where
this feature keeps all its arithmetic — on the server. `/recipes/:id/scale`
answers `totals = stored totals × target ÷ stored servings` from the **exact**
stored totals, and those totals *are* the diary row.

Two stated divergences from the donor, both forced by the deployed contract:
whole servings only (the route validates `z.number().int().min(1).max(100)`, so
the donor's 0.5 stepper would 400, and halving `per_serving` on the device is the
recompute this module forbids), and **no usage bump** (there is no
`/recipes/:id/use` route, and faking it with a PUT would re-resolve every
ingredient). These rows use the `HEALTH-RECIPE-*` ids the tests actually state,
so a row can be grepped straight into its case.

The device multiplied nothing | n/a (store) | `POST /health/recipes/:id/scale` → `POST /health/nutrition/entries` | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/health/__tests__/healthFoodStorage.test.ts` | **INTERACT** — rule 1 |

The donor derives from `caloriesPerServing`, which is **integer division**, so its 4→3→4 trip does not come back | n/a (store) | `scaleTotals` | n/a (local) | Unit | same | **CORNER** — the invariant |

### `HealthRecipesScreen` — 2026-07-29 Maestro pass, two gaps closed together (same screen)

`recipes-build-and-scale.yaml`'s own stated remaining gap ("the log-to-diary panel is now the largest remaining actionable gap... Next pass: split it") plus the 2026-07-27 "Nutrition parity push" fields (13-category picker, prep/cook time, searchable ingredient picker, photo attach) that shipped with zero Maestro coverage of any kind. Three new flows, each self-sufficient (mints and tears down its own ingredient/recipe, no ordering dependency on `recipes-build-and-scale.yaml` or each other).

---

## INJURY — injuries + activity preferences (P2 backend)

`backend/src/routes/health-body-extras.ts` + `backend/src/services/health-body-extras-service.ts` — the donor's `injuries.ts`, `activityPreferences.ts` and `bodyInsights.ts`, mounted at `/health` behind `requireHealthApi()`. Tables from migration 0120: `injuries`, `activity_notification_preferences`, `body_photo_insights`, `body_comprehensive_insights`.

Exercised by `backend/src/routes/__tests__/health-body-extras.test.ts` — **43 vitest cases**, runnable today: `npm --prefix backend test -- health-body-extras`.

`GET /injuries/active-body-parts` is a **safety surface**, not a report: it is the set a workout planner must suppress. Its failure modes are asymmetric, so both directions are scored explicitly —

- a **resolved** or **soft-deleted** injury must stop gating (INJ-027, INJ-028), otherwise a healed user is locked out of half their exercises forever;
- a **stale but still active** injury must keep gating (INJ-026), otherwise an age heuristic silently un-suppresses a part the user never healed. `pain_level: 0` is likewise not "healed" — only `resolve` or `delete` clears the gate (INJ-030).

`resolve` is deliberately **not** a delete: the row stays readable in history and in `?active=false` (INJ-019), and a flare-up can re-activate it (INJ-021).

Body insights are **read-only over HTTP** (INJ-036) — they are AI-produced in P3 and called in-process. A client-writable endpoint would let a device fabricate an "AI" body-composition assessment that is indistinguishable from the real analyser.

**The injury client shipped 2026-07-25.** `HealthInjuriesScreen` + `healthInjuryStorage.ts` +
`src/api/healthInjuries.ts` call all six `/injuries*` routes, closing the half of the
safety surface that had no writer: before this, the workout library's `injury_flag`
could only ever fire for a row entered outside the app. Its rows are `HEALTH-INJ-041`
… `073` below and `HEALTH-INJUI-001` … `017`. **Activity preferences and body insights
still have no client at all.** See [Harness gaps](#harness-gaps).

## INJUI — injury log screen

`src/features/health/screens/HealthInjuriesScreen.tsx` — the donor's injury tracker
(`BodyPainTrackerView` / `InjuriesTabContentView`), `defaultHidden` in the tab pool and
reached through the More hub. It is the **write half of the workout safety gate**: the
rows it creates are what `/health/exercises` reads to compute the `injury_flag` scored
under `EXER`.

Deliberate differences from the donor, each pinned by a row below: resolved injuries are
**visible and reopenable** (INJUI-013/014) where the donor hides them forever; the form is
**inline** rather than one of the donor's six stacked sheets (UI_PARITY_AUDIT §8); there is
a **date field** (INJUI-008) where the donor always stamps today; and there is **no body map
and no clinical muscle-group picker** — the gate reads neither, and letting a user tag a knee
ache "ACL" is self-diagnosis (INJUI-017).

Exercised by 17 Jest cases: `npx jest src/features/health/screens/__tests__/HealthInjuriesScreen.test.tsx`.
The pure store helpers are left REAL, so INJUI-005 asserts the actual wire string.

## ASSET — files, widget snapshot, fridge (P2 backend)

`backend/src/routes/health-assets.ts` + `backend/src/services/health-assets-service.ts` — the donor's `files.ts`, `widget.ts` and `fridge.ts`, mounted at `/health` behind `requireHealthApi()`. Tables from migration 0120: `user_files`, `widget_preferences`, `fridge_items`; bytes live in R2.

Exercised by `backend/src/routes/__tests__/health-assets.test.ts` — **86 vitest cases** against miniflare D1 **and** R2, so the byte-level assertions (upload, proxy, destroy) are real, not mocked: `npm --prefix backend test -- health-assets`.

Two rules are enforced in the service and asserted here:

- **`storage_key` never leaves the Worker.** R2 bindings have no S3-style presigned PUT, so — as in the household-photo contract — the client PUTs to a returned Worker path with its own bearer token and reads back through a proxied `content_path`. ASSET-010 sweeps **all three** file types (`photo`, `document`, `body_photo`) and greps the raw response text for the key, the bucket prefix, the column names and any absolute URL shaped like one. A leaked body-photo key is a privacy incident, so it is swept rather than sampled.
- **The widget snapshot carries a summary and only a summary.** A widget renders on a **locked** screen, so anything in that payload is readable without unlocking the device. ASSET-026 pins the key set literally, and ASSET-030 proves body photos, cycle and vitality data are all absent — a future field lands here as a failure first.

Deletes are soft for the sync cursor, but "delete this body photo" has to mean the **pixels** are gone: ASSET-017 asserts the R2 object is really destroyed while the D1 row survives as a tombstone.

Fridge validation deliberately mirrors the CHECK constraints of migration 0120 — D1 (miniflare's included) enforces them, so a missing zod rule is a 500 constraint error instead of an honest 400.

**No UI ships for any of this yet.** The RN widget still reads the App Group snapshot written by `HealthHomeScreen` (see WIDGET-022), not `GET /health/widget/snapshot`; there is no files screen and no fridge screen. See [Harness gaps](#harness-gaps).

---

## FRIDGE — Fridge tab (donor "Smart Fridge")

`HealthFridgeScreen` + `healthFridgeStorage` + `src/api/healthFridge.ts` — the UI over the already-deployed `/health/fridge` CRUD (see **ASSET** for the Worker contract). Food stock with quantity, unit, category, expiry, notes and favourites, grouped by expiry urgency, with an expiring-soon summary.

**The rule this surface exists to keep honest.** The deployed `?expiring_within_days=N` window is **inclusive of the past** and **excludes undated items** (`HealthAssetsService.listFridge`). So expired stock is the *most* urgent thing in the fridge and is rendered first, never folded into "this week" and never hidden; undated items get their own named group whose hint says outright that they can never appear in the expiring-soon count; and the summary prints the real cutoff date it counted to, so the number is checkable rather than magic.

`expiringWithin` is the offline twin of that server rule — if the two ever drift, the fridge starts silently hiding food that has gone off, which is why FRIDGE-005/006 and FRIDGE-031/032 pin both sides.

**Not ported:** barcode lookup, receipt OCR and AI meal suggestions. Those are donor capabilities needing a native scanner and a server-side model; neither exists on this Worker, and the screen does not pretend otherwise.

`HEALTH-FRIDGE-101 … 113` (added 2026-07-25, group 46) are the on-device half: `fridge-stock-and-expiry.yaml` stocks four of the five urgency buckets on a real account, reads the tiles and the cutoff note back, and empties the fridge again. `later` is the one bucket it does not stock — it carries no rule the other four are not already proving.

Save | Cancel returns the card to **ADD AN ITEM** and writes nothing; after the save the new name is on the row, the old one is gone, and the expiry the edit never touched still reads `Expires in 3 days` — a PUT that dropped the untouched columns would show up right here | `health-fridge-edit-*`, `health-fridge-cancel-edit` | `PUT /health/fridge/:id` | status 200 | Maestro | same | **INTERACT** / **CANCEL** — pairs with FRIDGE-022 |
| HEALTH-FRIDGE-113 | Teardown empties the fridge again | 1. Remove all four items, confirming each | Each removal raises a `Remove item` alert quoting the name, then the row is gone. The row action's label is `Remove <name>` and the alert's button is `Remove` — Maestro matches a label as one WHOLE string, so the two can never be confused. Stale stock would move the expiring-soon counts under the next run of this flow | `health-fridge-delete-*` | `DELETE /health/fridge/:id` ×4 | status 200 ×4 | Maestro | same | **MUTATION** — destructive teardown |

## CHAL — Food Challenges (donor `ChallengesSettingsView` + `WeeklyChallengeChartWidget`)

`HealthChallengesScreen` + `healthChallengesStorage` over the deployed
`/health/challenges` CRUD. A challenge is a target amount ("500g vegetables")
measured **daily** or **weekly**; the Worker matches logged foods against the
category — or, for `custom_ingredient`, against a named food — to compute
progress. The screen owns create / edit / pause / delete; the Home
`HealthFoodChallengesWidget` and the Nutrition tab's `HealthTodayChallengesCard`
only ever read.

**Why this section exists (added 2026-08-13).** The surface shipped with the
Dashboard parity phase carrying **zero coverage of any kind**. No Jest suite
named `HealthChallengesScreen.tsx` (490 lines), no Jest suite named
`healthChallengesStorage.ts` (282 lines) — it was reached only indirectly,
through `HealthTodayChallengesCard.test.tsx`, which mocks the loader outright
and so never let a single pure helper or a real `readThrough`/`writeThrough`
run — and no flow in `e2e/maestro/health/` drove any of the screen's fifteen
testIDs. This is the same class of hole the matrix's own 2026-07-31 `GOALS`
entry records, found the same way: by counting screens against test files rather
than trusting the Revision log.

**There is no tab, and no feature flag.** `health-challenges` is absent from
`src/config/healthFeatures.ts` entirely, so — unlike Cycle, Vitality, Fridge,
Injuries, Coach and Scan — it is **not** behind `needs-admin-feature-seed`: no
flag gates it, for an admin or a common user. It is also not in the tab pool for
anyone. The only two call sites that push it are the Home FOOD CHALLENGES
widget's pencil and the Nutrition tab's Today's-Challenges card. The pencil is
the entry the flow uses, because `HealthTodayChallengesCard` renders nothing
when no challenge applies today — the Nutrition route only exists once a
challenge already does.

**The rule this surface exists to keep honest.** `isChallengeOnTrack` judges a
week against its **elapsed share**, not its whole: the bar is
`(dayOfWeek / 7) × 0.8`, so a Monday needs ~11% banked and only a Sunday needs
the full 80%. A regression to a flat `0.8` would look plausible, pass every
render test, and tell almost every member they were "Behind" until Saturday.
`CHAL-020…023` pin both ends of that ramp.

`HEALTH-CHAL-001…037` are the store (`healthChallengesStorage.test.ts`);
`050…089` the screen (`HealthChallengesScreen.test.tsx`); `101…118` the device
half (`challenges-create-edit-delete.yaml`, groups 62–63).

## RESIL — partial payloads, defaulted "today", and optimistic merges

Added 2026-07-25 by the **unit-coverage pass** over the non-weight, non-food half
of `src/features/health`. Every row here is a path the shipped app can take that
the suite had never taken once. Three classes dominate, because all three are
invisible until they are not:

1. **A body with the payload key absent.** The Health Worker answers BARE
   (`c.json({ entries })`, no envelope), so a renamed key, a 204-shaped body or
   a route that changed arrives as `{}`. Every store already carried `?? []`;
   not one of those guards had ever been executed, and the one in
   `weightDailyValues` was a **lie** — it read `entries[0]` first.
2. **The defaulted `today` / `date`.** Every date helper takes the day as a
   parameter so the suite can pin the arithmetic, and every SCREEN call site
   omits it. The default is what decides whether the tab is talking about the
   right day at all.
3. **The optimistic merge.** Each writer paints its result into the list before
   the Worker replies, and OFFLINE that list is the only answer the screen ever
   gets. `editInjury`'s merge — eight decisions about which fields a patch
   overrides — had never run against a non-empty log.

| ID | Description | Steps | Expected | UI elements | BE / persistence | Console verify | Layer | Automation | Notes |
|----|-------------|-------|----------|-------------|------------------|----------------|-------|------------|-------|
| HEALTH-DASH-090…093 | A missing chart series draws EMPTY, never a row of zeroes | 1. Hand each Trends builder `undefined` in place of its series or its day keys | `[]` / `{ values: [], unit: null }` / `{ min: null, max: null }` / zero coverage without dividing by zero | Trends charts | none (pure) | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/health/__tests__/healthDashboards.test.ts` | **CORNER** — a zero series reads as "I logged 0", which is a different claim from "no data" |
| HEALTH-DASH-091 | `weightDailyValues` no longer crashes on a missing series | 1. Call it with `undefined` | `{ values: [], unit: null }` | weight chart | none (pure) | n/a (local) | Unit | same | **DEFECT FIXED** — it read `entries[0]` BEFORE its own `?? []`, so a partial payload threw a TypeError out of the render instead of drawing an empty chart |
| HEALTH-VITAL-030…032 | Vitality survives a bare body, a row with no id/timestamp, and the defaulted date | 1. `{}` from `/mens-health/entries` 2. A row with `id` and `updated_at` deleted 3. `saveVitalityEntry(patch)` with no date | Empty log, not a crash; the row falls back to the deterministic `vitality-<date>` id and an empty `loggedAt` so it scores as the neutral 50 rather than a phantom logged day; the write lands on TODAY | Men's Health | `/health/mens-health/*` | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/health/__tests__/healthVitalityStorage.test.ts` | **CORNER** — every screen call omits the date, so 032 is the path the app actually takes |
| HEALTH-CYCLE-044…050, 061 | Cycle survives truncated day keys, bare bodies and multi-day logs | 1. `daysBetween('2026-07', …)` and `daysBetween(…, '2026')` 2. `{}` from `/cycle/symptoms` and `/cycle/periods` 3. Three symptom days on the wire 4. `logPeriodDay(flow)` / `saveCycleSymptomEntry(patch)` / `createEmptySymptomEntry()` with no date | A missing month/day reads as the 1st rather than propagating NaN into the cycle DAY and the next-period prediction; empty logs, not crashes; entries come back newest-first and STAY that way after a save; the defaulted writes land on TODAY | Women's Health | `/health/cycle/*` | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/health/__tests__/healthCycleStorage.test.ts` | **CORNER** — every existing case had ≤1 row on the wire, which is exactly the size at which a broken comparator still looks correct |

Log three symptoms, two tied on count | Deterministic order, stable read to read | patterns card | none (pure) | n/a (local) | Unit | same | **CORNER** — Map insertion order would let two symptoms swap places for no reason the user can see |
| HEALTH-INJ-074…078 | The optimistic merge patches ONLY what the patch names | 1. Offline, two injuries cached 2. `editInjury(id, { pain_level })` 3. `{ cause: null }` 4. `{ notes: null }` 5. `{ pain_level: 9 }` 6. resolve then reopen | An absent key leaves the field alone; an explicit `null` CLEARS the cause and the clear sticks; a nulled note becomes empty text, never the string `null`; an out-of-scale level is ignored rather than stored; the row that was not touched is untouched | injuries list | `PUT /health/injuries/:id` | n/a (offline) | Unit | `src/features/health/__tests__/healthInjuryStorage.test.ts` | **CORNER** — the merge had NEVER run against a non-empty log; every lifecycle case ran on an empty one |

A row with every nullable column null 3. `humanizeInjuryToken` / `injuryCauseLabel` on unknown and blank tokens 4. A non-string in the date field 5. A note one character over the 1000 cap 6. A malformed `today` | Empty log; nulls become empty text / the defaults, never `"null"` in a `<Text>` or an uncontrolled `<TextInput>`; unknown causes read as words; the over-long note is refused with OUR sentence rather than a bare 400 from the Worker; the ago-label never reads `logged NaN days ago` | injury form + rows | `/health/injuries*` | n/a (local) | Unit | same | **CORNER** — no-raw-error-leak; the cap is inclusive, pinned both sides |

Call `daysUntil` / `expiryBucketOf` / `formatExpiry` / `quickPickDate` / `summarizeFridge` / `groupByExpiry` / `expiringWithin` / `viewFridge` / `loadExpiringSoon` with no `today` | All agree on the same day, and `loadExpiringSoon()` asks the route for `{ expiring_within_days: 7, today }` — the same 7 days the card's copy announces | fridge cards | `GET /health/fridge?expiring_within_days` | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/health/__tests__/healthFridgeStorage.test.ts` | **CORNER** — the screen omits `today` everywhere; the suite had never taken that arm |

An undated row inside the expiring window | An unparseable date reads as UNDATED, never as expired; inputs clear rather than throw; empty fridge, not a crash; a missing `source` is assumed manual so the row stays editable; ordering is by name so the list is stable read to read; blanks are stored as `null` / the default rather than `""`; a stray undated row sorts deterministically | fridge list | `/health/fridge*` | n/a (local) | Unit | same | **CORNER** — bucketing a bad date as `expired` would put "throw this out" next to food that is fine |

Tap the **No date** quick pick 3. Toggle favourite and save 4. Clear the search 5. Delete the row currently being EDITED 6. | HEALTH-MORE-048…054 | The Apple Health CONNECT flow, all four states | 1. Connect from `not-requested` 2. "Sync now" while already connected 3. A DENIED answer 4. Open Settings 5. ## HK — Apple Health bridge (read-only, opt-in, event-driven)

`src/features/health/healthKit.ts` (service) + `healthKitBridge.ts` (adapter) +
`healthKitBackgroundSync.ts` (event trigger) + `modules/symply-healthkit/` (Swift).
NINE scoped scalar read types (steps, active energy, sleep, heart rate, body mass,
dietary energy/protein/carbs/fat) plus workout sessions (`HKWorkoutType`, its own
sentinel identifier — no writes anywhere. `SymplyHealthKitModule.swift` runs
`HKObserverQuery` + `enableBackgroundDelivery` + `HKAnchoredObjectQuery` per scoped
type, so a change in Apple Health (including one written by a third-party app) can
trigger a sync without the user tapping Sync. Everything below runs in Jest against
a fake native module — **no row here needs a device**, which is the point: the only
things a real iPhone can add are the OS permission sheet, real sample data, and
actually exercising background delivery (which cannot be triggered on the Simulator
at all).

The whole surface is OPT-IN behind one button. `Apple Health — not connected` is
still the shipped state for every brand but Health, for Android, for the simulator,
and for any build without the entitlement — see HK-300…305 and HK-350…354.

| ID | Description | Steps | Expected | UI elements | BE / persistence | Console verify | Layer | Automation | Notes |
|----|-------------|-------|----------|-------------|------------------|----------------|-------|------------|-------|
| HEALTH-HK-300…305 | Only Symply Health gets a bridge | 1. Resolve the bridge for each brand / platform / module state | `null` for House, Budget, Kaizen, Language; `null` on Android; `null` with no linked module — and `null` falls through to `nullHealthKitBridge`, whose reason is `no-bridge` | connect card renders `unavailable` | none | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/health/__tests__/healthKitBridge.test.ts` | **PRIVACY** — four sibling apps share this `src/`; a bridge resolving on any of them is an incident, not a bug |
| HEALTH-HK-310…312 | Availability is reported, never assumed | 1. Module answers true / false / a non-boolean | Passed through; anything that is not literally `true` reads as unavailable | connect card state | none | n/a (local) | Unit | same | **CORNER** |
| HEALTH-HK-320…326 | The OS is only ever asked for the scoped set | 1. Pass extra identifiers 2. Have the module answer with extras and with junk | Out-of-scope identifiers are stripped before the call AND after it; an unrecognised status string is dropped rather than guessed, so it can never read as a grant | n/a | none | n/a (local) | Unit | same | **PRIVACY** — the permission footprint cannot widen from either side |
| HEALTH-HK-330…338, 355 | Nothing malformed is repaired | 1. Feed the adapter bad units, dates, values, windows and non-arrays | Every one is dropped. A unit mismatch in particular is dropped, not converted — guessing turns 70 kg into 70 lb in a weight log | n/a | none | n/a (local) | Unit | same | **CORNER** |
| HEALTH-HK-340…346 | The Swift keeps the TypeScript's promises, and DOES subscribe | 1. Read `SymplyHealthKitModule.swift` as text | Its allow-list is exactly `HEALTHKIT_READ_IDENTIFIERS`; no `save`, no workout BUILDER, no sample constructor (reading `HKWorkout` via `HKSampleQuery` is fine); `HKObserverQuery` / `enableBackgroundDelivery` / `HKAnchoredObjectQuery` ARE present and the completion handler is always called; a background-observed change posts a local notification only when `applicationState != .active`, with generic value-free copy; `consumePendingSync` reads/clears the same flag the observer sets; sleep counts `allAsleepValues` and never `.inBed` | n/a | none | n/a (static) | Unit | same | **PRIVACY** — two hand-written copies of the same identifiers; drift on one side only is exactly how scope widens unreviewed |

Read the entitlements pair, `Info.plist`, `Brand.xcconfig` and `project.pbxproj` | HealthKit AND background-delivery appear ONLY in `SymplyEcosystem-Health*.entitlements`, which only `Debug-health` / `Release-health` reference; `$(SYMPLY_HEALTHKIT_SHARE_USAGE)` / `UPDATE_USAGE` are non-empty on EVERY brand (App Store Connect validator 90683 requires the keys whenever the linked binary references HealthKit APIs, regardless of entitlement) — House/Budget/Kaizen/Language get a generic "does not read/write" default, only Health's two configs override it with real copy; still read-only (no write identifier, no `save`) | n/a | none | n/a (static) | Unit | same | **PRIVACY** — HealthKit in the shared entitlements would break four apps' code signing AND make them ask for health data |

| HEALTH-HK-400…409 | The event-driven trigger: debounced, both arrival paths, safe teardown | 1. Fire the native change event once and several times close together 2. Resolve `consumePendingSync() → true` on mount and on every `AppState` → `active` 3. Call `stop()` mid-debounce | No native module ⇒ safe no-op; a burst of events collapses into ONE `importNow()` after the debounce window; a successful import with changes shows a toast summarising what changed, an import with nothing new shows nothing, a rejected import never throws; the pending-flag path covers a change that landed with no live JS runtime, checked on mount AND on every foreground; `stop()` removes the listener and cancels a pending debounce | toast (`showToast`) | none (fake `healthKit.importNow`) | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/health/__tests__/healthKitBackgroundSync.test.ts` | **INTERACT** — this is the "no schedulers, no polling, event-driven" contract; the debounce IS the only timer in this file, and it only ever starts in response to a signal |

## AI — coach, scanners, body-insight producer (P3)

`backend/src/routes/health-ai.ts` + `backend/src/services/health-ai/**` (`coach-safety.ts`,
`coach-insights.ts`, `coach-proposals.ts`, `coach-service.ts`, `vision-service.ts`,
`measurement-insight-service.ts`), the prompts in `backend/src/ai/prompts/health-{coach,nutrition-label,meal-photo}.ts`,
and migration `backend/migrations/0128_health_ai.sql` (2 tables). On the client:
`src/api/healthAi.ts`, `src/features/health/healthCoachStorage.ts`,
`src/features/health/screens/HealthCoachScreen.tsx` and
`src/features/health/screens/HealthScanScreen.tsx`.

Ports three donor surfaces: the AI coach (Health Coach V2 — turn, commit,
operation ledger, consent receipts — folded into `routes/health-ai.ts` above,
**not** a separate `routes/healthCoach.ts`, which does not exist in this repo;
2026-07-31: corrected, this paragraph and the 2026-07-26 Revision entry below
both named the wrong file), `POST /ai/scan-nutrition-label` (+ its second
`/scan-product-name` pass), and `POST /ai/analyze-food` / `/ai/analyze-food-scale`.
Plus the **body-insight producer** `routes/health-body-extras.ts` has been waiting for
since P2 ("the producer lands in P3 and calls the service in-process").

**THE MODEL IS MOCKED IN EVERY CASE BELOW.** No test in this section reaches a provider,
including the ones that assert what happens on a timeout, a refusal, or a media-type
mismatch. Run them with
`npm --prefix backend test -- health-ai` and
`npx jest src/features/health/__tests__/healthCoachStorage src/features/health/screens/__tests__/Health{Coach,Scan}Screen`.

Three properties carry the section, because each fails silently:

- **ESCALATION IS DETERMINISTIC AND RUNS FIRST.** `coach-safety.ts` matches eleven
  emergency categories by regex *before* consent, entitlement or any model call. The
  donor has the same matcher and its own LLM path never reaches it — on any donor
  account with AI configured, "chest pain and I can't breathe" goes straight to a model
  whose only guardrail is the prompt line `- Never provide medical advice.` AI-120 is a
  hard negative on the provider mock, with entitlement rigged to deny and consent absent.
- **GROUNDING IS MECHANICAL, NOT PROMPTED.** `composeInsightSpeech` throws
  `ungrounded_number:<n>` for any sentence containing a numeral the insight's own `facts`
  do not hold, and the body-insight producer runs the model's prose through the same
  guard, dropping what fails (AI-011, AI-177). A prompt asking a model not to invent a
  figure is a request; this is a refusal.
- **THE COACH CANNOT WRITE.** It emits a proposal carrying an FNV-1a `payload_hash` over
  the exact numbers shown; the commit route re-hashes what it receives AND requires the
  client to echo the hash it displayed. AI-045 proves a tampered payload is refused even
  with a matching echo, and AI-040 proves tampering outranks staleness so a changed
  payload can never read as a mere timing problem.

Deliberate differences from the donor, each pinned: **English-only** escalation copy
(the donor is bilingual EN/RU; the app ships English, as `0123` decided when it dropped
`name_ru`) with **word-boundary anchors** so "I am dying to know how many calories I have
left" does not trip the self-harm rule (AI-002); **deny-by-default consent** where the
donor auto-grants five scopes on first use and says so in a comment (AI-110, AI-113);
**no conversation table** — the donor does not persist transcripts either, so the
transcript lives under `health.coach.v1` and is cleared on sign-out; and **no body-fat
estimate and no posture score** on the produced insight, because there is no photograph
and a tape measure cannot produce either (AI-172).

| ID | Description | Steps | Expected | UI elements | BE / persistence | Console verify | Layer | Automation | Notes |
|----|-------------|-------|----------|-------------|------------------|----------------|-------|------------|-------|
| HEALTH-AI-001 | Every escalation category is reachable from a phrase a person would type | 1. Match one real sentence per category | All eleven fire, and the set of categories reached equals the set of rules declared — an orphaned rule is one nobody has read since it was written | n/a | `coach-safety.ts` | n/a (pure) | Unit | `backend/src/services/health-ai/__tests__/coach-safety.test.ts` | **CORNER** — safety surface, both directions scored |
| HEALTH-AI-002 | Ordinary logging and gym talk never escalates | 1. Feed ten near-miss phrases ("my chest workout was hard", "I am dying to know how many calories I have left", "I want to cut my calories") | None escalates. A false positive replaces a real answer with an emergency notice and teaches the person to ignore it | n/a | `coach-safety.ts` | n/a (pure) | Unit | same | **CORNER** — the donor's bare substrings trip on several of these |
| HEALTH-AI-003 | Every message says help was NOT contacted, in the copy and in the flag | 1. Read all eleven messages | `claims_help_contacted: false`, `collect_location: false`, and the sentence says it in words | n/a | `coach-safety.ts` | n/a (pure) | Unit | same | **VISIBLE** — an app that leaves someone believing help is coming is worse than silence |
| HEALTH-AI-004 | No escalation message diagnoses, prescribes or names a treatment | 1. Grep all eleven for the clinical register | No "you have <condition>", no dose, no RICE, no "ice it". Each states a REASON TO SEEK CARE, which is not a finding | n/a | `coach-safety.ts` | n/a (pure) | Unit | same | **CORNER** — the donor's siblings claim osteopathy expertise and write a "Clinical summary" |
| HEALTH-AI-005 | The one medication sentence points at an EXISTING prescription | 1. Read the anaphylaxis message | It says "if you have been prescribed one" — conditional, referring to a clinician's prescription. It names no drug and gives no dose | n/a | `coach-safety.ts` | n/a (pure) | Unit | same | **CORNER** — the single place naming a medication is right, pinned so an edit cannot turn it into instructions |

---

## BEHARDEN — the Health BACKEND coverage pass (2026-07-26)

Every row below is a **backend** row: it runs under `npm --prefix backend test`
with no simulator, no session and no network. They were written by working down
the uncovered-branch list for the Health Worker surface (`routes/health*.ts`,
`services/health*.ts`, `services/health-ai/**`), so each one exists because a
real branch had never been executed — not because a shape looked untested.

**Four product defects were found this way and fixed in the same pass.** They are
marked **DEFECT FIXED** and each names what a member would have seen.

The IDs stay inside their existing families (`NUTR`, `CYCLE`, `ACT`, `AI`,
`FOOD`, `SYNC`, `PRIV`) so a reader chasing one domain finds them where they
expect; they are grouped here because they landed together.

### 2026-07-26 — the Home tab could not be scrolled, and its "Done" bar did not exist

**DEFECT FIXED — `HealthHomeScreen.tsx`.** Two symptoms, one cause.

`HealthHomeScreen` mounted two `InputAccessoryView`s unconditionally on iOS, to
hang a **Done** button off the weight field's decimal pad (which has no return
key) and off the note field. Under the New Architecture that component renders
nothing: **the bar never appeared.** A member typing a weight saw no Done, and
`home-keyboard-accessories.yaml` failed on `health-weight-keyboard-done` "is
visible" — a flow that looked like a stale assertion and was reporting a real
absence.

The second symptom was worse and had been mis-attributed for weeks. The mounted
native accessory container **swallowed the pan gesture for the entire screen**,
so the Home ScrollView could not be scrolled at all — not by swipe, not by
`scrollUntilVisible`, not by drag — while taps went through normally. Measured on
com.symply.health / iOS 26 on 2026-07-26: four 50%-height swipes produced six
byte-identical screenshots. With the two elements commented out and nothing else
changed, `scrollUntilVisible` COMPLETED on the first attempt. **A person on a
phone could not scroll their own Home tab past AT A GLANCE** — the WATER counter,
TODAY'S NOTE, RECENT ACTIVITY, the SECTIONS grid and the privacy card were all
unreachable.

The matrix had recorded this as an iOS-26 Maestro limitation. It was not: every
other Health screen scrolls under the same driver, and the only structural
difference between `HealthHomeScreen` and `HealthSectionScreen` was this pair.

**The fix.** Both bars are now ordinary rows inside their own cards, rendered
while the field owns the keyboard (`onFocus` / `onBlur` state, iOS only — the
Android keyboard has a return key and `onSubmitEditing` already commits). Same
testIDs, same behaviour, and they render. The ScrollView also gained
`automaticallyAdjustKeyboardInsets`, because the keypad was covering the row that
summoned it: the log row sits at y≈569pt and the decimal pad's top edge lands at
≈568pt, so the "+" that the card's own copy points at ("Log your first weight
below") was half under the keyboard. `health-home-scroll` was added at the same
time — every other scroll container in the feature was addressable and Home was
the exception.

Pinned by `HEALTH-HOME-067`, `-067b` (a regression guard asserting **no**
`InputAccessoryView` is mounted and no `inputAccessoryViewID` is set), `-068`,
`-069`, `-070` and `-070b`. `src/features/health` stays at 100% statements,
branches, functions and lines.

### The tombstone contract — a deleted row is indistinguishable from one that never existed

| ID | Description | Steps | Expected | UI elements | BE / persistence | Console verify | Layer | Automation | Notes |
|----|-------------|-------|----------|-------------|------------------|----------------|-------|------------|-------|
| HEALTH-PRIV-030 | Every EDIT route 404s a row the caller already deleted | 1. Create a weight, a diary row and a workout 2. Delete all three 3. `PUT`/`POST` each of the five edit paths | 404 on all five. Unknown id, another user's id and a tombstone must answer identically, or a probe gains a third signal | n/a (route) | `weight_entries`, `nutrition_entries`, `health_entries` | n/a (route) | Unit | `backend/src/routes/__tests__/health.test.ts` | **DEFECT FIXED** — `updateWeight` and `updateNutrition` looked up by `(id, user_id)` with no `deleted_at` guard, so a member's own tombstone answered **200 with a body** while every other miss answered 404. `reportionNutrition` and `readOwnedEntry` already documented the rule in their own comments |
| HEALTH-PRIV-031 | A refused edit writes NOTHING — same stamp, same figure | 1. Delete a weight 2. `PUT` a new figure 3. ### Back-dating, the metric switch, and the session sheet's dismiss (E2E, 2026-08-13)

The five Activity controls neither `activity-log-workout.yaml` nor
`activity-goals-and-session-edit.yaml` ever touched — the whole date/time half
of the log form — plus `health-workout-detail-close`. The rule worth a flow is
`HealthActivityScreen.tsx:713`: the date is gated on
`/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/ && form.date <= today`, so a workout **cannot** be logged
in the future and the field refuses it in words rather than silently correcting
it. The fields exist for the after-midnight case (a session finished at 00:30
would otherwise land on the wrong day); a back-dating control that accepts 2099
is one that will also accept a typo'd year on a real entry.

| ID | Description | Steps | Expected | UI elements | BE / persistence | Console verify | Layer | Automation | Notes |
|----|-------------|-------|----------|-------------|------------------|----------------|-------|------------|-------|
| HEALTH-ACT-180 | The screen mounts with the log form on it, from a normalised state | 1. Open Activity 2. Delete any stale `Cycling` session | `health-activity-screen` + **LOG A WORKOUT**; no leftover session from an aborted run | screen | `GET /health/entries` | n/a (route) | Maestro | `e2e/maestro/health/activity-datetime-and-steps-metric.yaml` | **LOAD** — MOUNT stage; the guard is optional at every step, since the common case is nothing to clean |
| HEALTH-ACT-181…182 | STEPS & DISTANCE switches the SERIES, not just the chip | 1. Tap **Distance** 2. Tap **Steps** | The steps-only empty/chart pair (`health-activity-steps-empty`) is absent while Distance is selected, and returns after | `health-activity-steps-metric-steps`, `-distance` | none (local derive) | n/a (UI-only) | Maestro | same | **INTERACT** — asserting the chip's own selected state alone would pass against a control that repainted and showed nothing new |
| HEALTH-ACT-183…184 | A workout cannot be logged in the FUTURE | 1. Type `2099-01-01` 2. Press Add | `health-workout-datetime-error` reads "a workout cannot be in the future"; the press does nothing and the error survives | `health-workout-date-input`, `health-workout-add-button` | none — `canLog` is false | n/a (UI-only) | Maestro | same | **VALIDATION** — BEHAVIOURAL: Maestro's `enabled:` filter does not match a disabled RN `Pressable` on this build (2026-07-26) |
| HEALTH-ACT-185 | The time refuses a non-clock | 1. Type `99:99` | "Use a 24-hour time like 07:30." | `health-workout-time-input` | none | n/a (UI-only) | Maestro | same | **VALIDATION** — both date/time fields are `numbers-and-punctuation`, a full keyboard WITH a return key, so `hideKeyboard` is safe here (unlike the minutes number-pad below it) |
| HEALTH-ACT-186…188 | The quick chips repair both fields | 1. **Now** 2. **Today** 3. **Yesterday** | Each chip WRITES a valid value into the field it owns; the error clears and stays clear. Yesterday is the chip the after-midnight case actually needs | `health-workout-time-now`, `health-workout-date-today`, `health-workout-date-yesterday` | none (draft) | n/a (UI-only) | Maestro | same | **INTERACT** — the chips are why a member never has to type either field correctly by hand |

Tap close | The sheet closes and **Cycling** is still on the list | `health-workout-detail-close` | none | n/a (UI-only) | Maestro | same | **CANCEL** — the only flow that opens this sheet goes straight to Delete, so the dismiss path a member takes every time they merely look at a session was unproven |

Confirm | `Delete Cycling?`, then the row is gone and it really left the server | `health-workout-detail-delete` | `DELETE /health/entries/:id` | `e2e-verify-network` (`DELETE /entries/` → `200`) | Maestro | same | **MUTATION** — destructive teardown; the row is matched on the id pattern AND its own label |

| ID | Description | Steps | Expected | UI elements | BE / persistence | Console verify | Layer | Automation | Notes |

### 2026-07-26 — the P3 AI surface, on a device

Until this pass `AI` held 173 scored rows across backend vitest and Jest and
**zero** E2E: `HealthCoachScreen` and `HealthScanScreen` were entirely unproven
on a device. Two flows close that gap. Both tabs are `defaultHidden` (AI is
opt-in for this app), so each enters through the More hub and pins its `tab-*`
negative.

What is asserted HARD and what BRANCHES is a deliberate split. The consent gate,
the composer's inert states, the transcript's empty state, Clear, the scan mode
picker and the Drive picker are deterministic and asserted hard. The MODEL's
answer is not: a turn comes back as a reply, as a reply carrying a proposal, or
as a banner when the provider could not be reached, and the screen has honest
copy for all three. A flow that demanded a particular sentence from an LLM would
be red for reasons that are not regressions.

| ID | Description | Steps | Expected | UI elements | BE / persistence | Console verify | Layer | Automation | Notes |
